409:         outcome: failures.length === 0 ? "passed" : "failed",
410:         // Caller attribution verified separately against archives/consumer; not child-attested.
411:         declaredSourceCommit: sourceCommit,
412:         workspaceDirty,
413:         entry: resolvedEntry,
414:         entrySha256,
415:         binding: binding ?? null,
416:         artifactBytes:
417:           artifacts.descriptors.reduce((n, d) => n + Buffer.byteLength(d.utf8), 0) +
418:           artifacts.schemas.reduce((n, s) => n + Buffer.byteLength(s.utf8), 0),
419:         deadlineExceeded,
420:         attempted,
421:         exchanges: responses.length,
422:         completedResponses: responses,
423:         closed,
424:         startupCompleted: child !== undefined,
425:         childPid: child?.child.pid ?? null,
426:         childExitCode: child?.child.exitCode ?? null,
427:         childSignalCode: child?.child.signalCode ?? null,
428:         directory: directory ?? null,
429:         directoryAbsent: directory === undefined || !existsSync(directory),
430:         failureCount: failures.length,
431:         claimEligible: false,
432:         retention: "response fingerprints and selected headers; full body assertions ran in memory",
433:       }),
434:     );
435:   }
436:   if (failures.length === 1) throw failures[0];
437:   if (failures.length > 1)
438:     throw new AggregateError(failures, "installed changefeed observation and cleanup failed");
439: }, 60000);

===== ACTUAL FULL SOURCE packages/server/src/read-http.ts =====
1: import { stringifyJsonValue } from "@bdp/protocol";
2: import type { HttpResponse } from "./index.js";
3: 
4: /** Apply shared HTTP rules only after routing, authorization and result validation.
5:  * This Read server selects parameter-free application/json on its 200 routes.
6:  * Its Scope port supplies no representation modification date, so neither date
7:  * precondition can be evaluated; Resource timestamps are not HTTP validators.
8:  * RFC 9110 sections 12.5.1, 13.1 and 13.2 govern negotiation and ordering.
9:  */
10: export function applyReadHttpSemantics(request: Request, response: HttpResponse): HttpResponse {
11:   let selected = response;
12:   const json =
13:     response.status === 200 && response.headers.get("content-type") === "application/json";
14:   if (json || response.status === 204) {
15:     const headers = new Headers(response.headers);
16:     if (json) {
17:       const vary = headers.get("vary");
18:       if (
19:         vary !== "*" &&
20:         !vary?.split(",").some((field) => field.trim().toLowerCase() === "accept")
21:       )
22:         headers.set("vary", vary ? `${vary}, Accept` : "Accept");
23:     }
24:     // The Scope probe is an existing zero-length representation with no media
25:     // type to negotiate; it still evaluates entity-tag preconditions.
26:     const status =
27:       json && !acceptsJson(request.headers.get("accept"))
28:         ? 406
29:         : conditionalReadStatus(request.headers, headers.get("etag"));
30:     if (status !== 200) {
31:       headers.delete("content-type");
32:       if (status === 406) headers.delete("etag");
33:       // A 304 Content-Length may describe only the corresponding 200 body,
34:       // never an invented zero-length representation (RFC 9110 section 8.6).
35:       headers.delete("content-length");
36:       if (status !== 304) headers.set("content-length", "0");
37:       selected = { status, headers };
38:     } else selected = { ...response, headers };
39:   }
40:   // Keep the Fetch-level contract honest as well as the Node transport's final
41:   // defensive strip. HEAD has GET metadata, including the serialized length.
42:   if (request.method === "HEAD" && selected.body !== undefined) {
43:     const headers = new Headers(selected.headers);
44:     headers.set("content-length", String(Buffer.byteLength(stringifyJsonValue(selected.body))));
45:     return { status: selected.status, headers };
46:   }
47:   return selected;
48: }
49: 
50: export function conditionalReadStatus(headers: Headers, etag: string | null): 200 | 304 | 412 {
51:   const match = headers.get("if-match");
52:   if (match !== null && !tagListMatches(match, etag, true)) return 412;
53:   const noneMatch = headers.get("if-none-match");
54:   if (noneMatch !== null && tagListMatches(noneMatch, etag, false)) return 304;
55:   return 200;
56: }
57: 
58: /** A tag's backslash is an opaque byte, not a quoted-string escape. Empty list
59:  * elements are ignored under RFC 9110 section 5.6.1; mixed '*' lists are invalid.
60:  * Invalid fields cannot produce a match (13.1.1/13.1.2's 'otherwise' branches).
61:  */
62: function tagListMatches(value: string, current: string | null, strong: boolean): boolean {
63:   if (value.trim() === "*") return true; // Called only for an existing successful representation.
64:   const tag = /(?:W\/)?"[\x21\x23-\x7e\x80-\xff]*"/y;
65:   let offset = 0;
66:   let matched = false;
67:   while (offset < value.length) {
68:     while (/[\t ,]/.test(value[offset] ?? "") && offset < value.length) offset += 1;
69:     if (offset === value.length) break;
70:     tag.lastIndex = offset;
71:     const parsed = tag.exec(value);
72:     if (parsed === null) return false;
73:     const candidate = parsed[0];
74:     if (current !== null) {
75:       matched ||= strong
76:         ? !candidate.startsWith("W/") && !current.startsWith("W/") && candidate === current
77:         : candidate.replace(/^W\//, "") === current.replace(/^W\//, "");
78:     }
79:     offset = tag.lastIndex;
80:     while (value[offset] === " " || value[offset] === "\t") offset += 1;
81:     if (offset < value.length && value[offset] !== ",") return false;
82:   }
83:   return matched;
84: }
85: 
86: const TOKEN = /^[!#$%&'*+.^_`|~0-9A-Za-z-]+$/;
87: const QUOTED = /^"(?:[\t\x20\x21\x23-\x5b\x5d-\x7e\x80-\xff]|\\[\t\x20-\x7e\x80-\xff])*"$/;
88: const QUALITY = /^(?:0(?:\.\d{0,3})?|1(?:\.0{0,3})?)$/;
89: 
90: export function acceptsJson(value: string | null): boolean {
91:   return mediaQuality(value, "application/json") > 0;
92: }
93: 
94: /** Source-private representation selection; JSON wins ties and missing Accept. */
95: export function selectChangefeedMedia(value: string | null): "json" | "sse" | undefined {
96:   const json = mediaQuality(value, "application/json");
97:   const sse = mediaQuality(value, "text/event-stream");
98:   return json > 0 && json >= sse ? "json" : sse > 0 ? "sse" : undefined;
99: }
100: 
101: function mediaQuality(value: string | null, target: string): number {
102:   if (value === null) return 1;
103:   const ranges = splitQuoted(value, ",");
104:   if (ranges === undefined) return 0;
105:   let specificity = -1;
106:   let quality = 0;
107:   for (const range of ranges) {
108:     const parsed = mediaRange(range, target);
109:     if (parsed === undefined) continue;
110:     if (parsed.specificity > specificity) {
111:       specificity = parsed.specificity;
112:       quality = parsed.quality;
113:     } else if (parsed.specificity === specificity) quality = Math.max(quality, parsed.quality);
114:   }
115:   return quality;
116: }
117: 
118: /** Unsupported media parameters cannot match our parameter-free JSON. Malformed
119:  * ranges are not candidates; no BDP error code is invented for header recovery.
120:  */
121: function mediaRange(
122:   value: string,
123:   target: string,
124: ): { specificity: number; quality: number } | undefined {
125:   const parts = splitQuoted(value, ";");
126:   if (parts === undefined) return undefined;
127:   const media = parts.shift()?.trim().toLowerCase();
128:   const specificity =
129:     media === target ? 2 : media === `${target.split("/")[0]}/*` ? 1 : media === "*/*" ? 0 : -1;
130:   if (specificity < 0) return undefined;
131:   let quality = 1;
132:   let hasWeight = false;
133:   let hasMediaParameters = false;
134:   for (const part of parts) {
135:     const parameter = part.trim();
136:     if (parameter === "") continue;
137:     const equal = parameter.indexOf("=");
138:     if (equal < 0) return undefined;
139:     const name = parameter.slice(0, equal);
140:     const raw = parameter.slice(equal + 1);
141:     if (!TOKEN.test(name) || (!TOKEN.test(raw) && !QUOTED.test(raw))) return undefined;
142:     if (name.toLowerCase() === "q") {
143:       if (hasWeight || !QUALITY.test(raw)) return undefined;
144:       quality = Number(raw);
145:       hasWeight = true;
146:     } else hasMediaParameters = true;
147:   }
148:   return hasMediaParameters ? undefined : { specificity, quality };
149: }
150: 
151: /** Split HTTP media syntax without mistaking quoted commas/semicolons for
152:  * separators. Quoted-pairs apply here, unlike the entity-tag grammar above.
153:  */
154: function splitQuoted(value: string, delimiter: string): string[] | undefined {
155:   const parts: string[] = [];
156:   let quoted = false;
157:   let start = 0;
158:   for (let index = 0; index < value.length; index += 1) {
159:     const character = value[index];
160:     if (quoted && character === "\\") index += 1;
161:     else if (character === '"') quoted = !quoted;
162:     else if (!quoted && character === delimiter) {
163:       parts.push(value.slice(start, index));
164:       start = index + 1;
165:     }
166:   }
167:   if (quoted) return undefined;
168:   parts.push(value.slice(start));
169:   return parts;
170: }
171: 
172: export function resourceRevisionEtag(revision: string): string {
173:   // RFC 9110 entity tags are quoted opaque byte sequences, not HTTP quoted-
174:   // strings: a quote or control byte cannot be escaped inside the tag. BDP's
175:   // ordinary ASCII revisions are emitted unchanged as shown in the spec. For
176:   // revisions outside the entity-tag character profile, use an unambiguous
177:   // base64url projection whose marker cannot collide with an unchanged tag.
178:   const bytes = Buffer.from(revision, "utf8");
179:   const unchanged =
180:     bytes.length === revision.length &&
181:     bytes.every((byte) => byte === 0x21 || (byte >= 0x23 && byte <= 0x7e));
182:   let opaque: string;
183:   if (unchanged && !revision.startsWith("bdp-b64_") && !revision.startsWith("bdp-u16_")) {
184:     opaque = revision;
185:   } else if (isWellFormedUnicode(revision)) {
186:     opaque = `bdp-b64_${bytes.toString("base64url")}`;
187:   } else {
188:     // JavaScript can represent lone UTF-16 surrogates, for which UTF-8's
189:     // replacement encoding is not injective. Preserve those otherwise-valid
190:     // JSON strings with a separate code-unit projection.
191:     const codeUnits = Buffer.allocUnsafe(revision.length * 2);
192:     for (let index = 0; index < revision.length; index += 1) {
193:       codeUnits.writeUInt16BE(revision.charCodeAt(index), index * 2);
194:     }
195:     opaque = `bdp-u16_${codeUnits.toString("base64url")}`;
196:   }
197:   return `"${opaque}"`;
198: }
199: 
200: function isWellFormedUnicode(value: string): boolean {
201:   for (let index = 0; index < value.length; index += 1) {
202:     const code = value.charCodeAt(index);
203:     if (code >= 0xd800 && code <= 0xdbff) {
204:       const following = value.charCodeAt(index + 1);
205:       if (index + 1 >= value.length || following < 0xdc00 || following > 0xdfff) return false;
206:       index += 1;
207:     } else if (code >= 0xdc00 && code <= 0xdfff) {
208:       return false;
209:     }
210:   }
211:   return true;
212: }

66: async function receive(request: IncomingMessage): Promise<TransactionReceivedBody> {
67:   const length = request.headers["content-length"];
68:   if (
69:     length !== undefined &&
70:     (!/^\d+$/.test(length) || Number(length) > TRANSACTION_RUNTIME_LIMITS.requestBodyBytes)
71:   )
72:     return { failure: "request-too-large" };
73:   const chunks: Buffer[] = [];
74:   let total = 0;
75:   for await (const chunk of request.iterator({ destroyOnReturn: false })) {
76:     total += chunk.length;
77:     if (total > TRANSACTION_RUNTIME_LIMITS.requestBodyBytes)
78:       return { failure: "request-too-large" };
79:     chunks.push(chunk);
80:   }
81:   try {
82:     return { text: new TextDecoder("utf-8", { fatal: true }).decode(Buffer.concat(chunks)) };
83:   } catch {
84:     return { failure: "malformed-request" };
85:   }
86: }
87: function deliver(response: ServerResponse, selected: HttpResponse, raw?: string): void {
88:   if (response.destroyed) return;
89:   const bytes =
90:     raw ?? (selected.body === undefined ? undefined : stringifyJsonValue(selected.body));
91:   selected.headers.set("x-bdp-development", "non-attesting");
92:   if (bytes !== undefined) selected.headers.set("content-length", String(Buffer.byteLength(bytes)));
93:   response.writeHead(selected.status, Object.fromEntries(selected.headers));
94:   response.end(response.req.method === "HEAD" ? undefined : bytes);
95: }
96: export async function startTransactionRuntime(
97:   options: TransactionRuntimeOptions,
98:   seams: TransactionRuntimeTestSeams = {},
99: ) {
100:   const captured = captureTransactionRuntimeConfiguration(options, seams);
101:   const { settings, configuredScope, contracts, types } = captured;
102:   const capturedSeams = captured.seams;
103:   let service: ReturnType<typeof createTransactionService> | undefined;
104:   let handler: ReturnType<typeof createTransactionHttpHandler> | undefined;
105:   let schemas: ReturnType<typeof createTransactionSchemaHttp> | undefined;
106:   let configurationMetadata: ReturnType<typeof captured.metadata>;
107:   let listenUrl = "";
108:   let scope = "",
109:     closing = false;
110:   const pending = new Set<Promise<void>>();
111:   // Retain only the first unexpected live fault. Wire cancellation remains
112:   // silent; close surfaces the original cause through existing aggregation.
113:   let firstStreamFault: { readonly cause: unknown } | undefined;
114:   const handle = async (request: IncomingMessage, response: ServerResponse): Promise<void> => {
115:     let signal: AbortSignal | undefined;
116:     let streamContext: TransactionReadContext | undefined;
117:     try {
118:       if (closing || !handler) {
119:         deliver(response, transactionHttpProblem("temporarily-unavailable"));
120:         return;
121:       }
122:       const raw = request.url ?? "";
123:       if (request.method === "POST" && Buffer.byteLength(raw) > TRANSACTION_HTTP_TARGET_BYTES) {
124:         deliver(response, transactionHttpProblem("request-too-large"));
125:         return;
126:       }
127:       if (
128:         normalizationSafeRawRequestPath(raw) === undefined ||
129:         !raw.startsWith("/") ||
130:         raw.startsWith("//") ||
131:         request.headers.host !==
132:           new URL(captured.authorityMode === "canonical" ? scope : listenUrl).host
133:       ) {
134:         deliver(response, transactionHttpProblem("malformed-request"));
135:         return;
136:       }
137:       const url = new URL(raw, scope);
138:       const methodFailure = handler.methodResponse(url, request.method ?? "GET");
139:       if (methodFailure) {
140:         // Refused bodies are not consumed and cannot keep this connection alive.
141:         methodFailure.headers.set("connection", "close");
142:         deliver(response, methodFailure);
143:         return;
144:       }
145:       const headers = new Headers();
146:       for (let i = 0; i < request.rawHeaders.length; i += 2)
147:         headers.append(request.rawHeaders[i] ?? "", request.rawHeaders[i + 1] ?? "");
148:       const supplied = Buffer.from(headers.get("authorization") ?? "");
149:       const authorization = captured.authenticate(supplied);
150:       signal = createNodeHttpDisconnectSignal(request, response);
151:       const input = new Request(url, { method: request.method ?? "GET", headers, signal });
152:       const lastEventIds: string[] = [];
153:       for (let i = 0; i < request.rawHeaders.length; i += 2)
154:         if (request.rawHeaders[i]?.toLowerCase() === "last-event-id")
155:           lastEventIds.push(request.rawHeaders[i + 1] ?? "");
156:       const body = request.method === "POST" ? await receive(request) : { text: "" };
157:       if (closing || response.destroyed) {
158:         deliver(response, transactionHttpProblem("temporarily-unavailable"));
159:         return;
160:       }
161:       // This fixture has no CORS policy. Origin is a transport refusal, not a
162:       // mutation permission synthesized from carried provenance.
163:       if (headers.has("origin")) {
164:         deliver(response, transactionHttpProblem("forbidden"));
165:         return;
166:       }
167:       if ("failure" in body) response.setHeader("connection", "close");
168:       const schema = await schemas?.(input, authorization);
169:       if (schema) {
170:         deliver(response, schema.response, schema.raw);
171:         return;
172:       }
173:       let selected: TransactionHttpResult | undefined = await handler.handle(
174:         input,
175:         authorization,
176:         body,
177:         lastEventIds,
178:       );
179:       if (selected.kind === "finite") deliver(response, selected);
180:       else {
181:         if (!captured.liveStreamsAvailable) {
182:           deliver(response, {
183:             status: 500,
184:             headers: new Headers({
185:               "cache-control": "private, no-store",
186:               "x-bdp-development-limit": "stream-transport-timing",
187:             }),
188:           });
189:           return;
190:         }
191:         const activeService = service!;
192:         streamContext = selected.context;
193:         await activeService.stream(async (streamSignal) => {
194:           try {
195:             streamSignal.throwIfAborted();
196:             if (!selected || selected.kind === "finite")
197:               throw new Error("missing stream selection");
198:             response.writeHead(selected.status, Object.fromEntries(selected.headers));
199:             response.flushHeaders();
200:             const pumping =
201:               selected.kind === "changefeed-stream"
202:                 ? pumpTransactionChanges(
203:                     response,
204:                     selected.page,
205:                     async (after, nextSignal) => {
206:                       const observed = await activeService.changes(
207:                         { after },
208:                         undefined,
209:                         nextSignal,
210:                       );
211:                       if (observed.kind !== "page")
212:                         throw new Error("changefeed stream lost serving entry");
213:                       return observed.page;
214:                     },
215:                     streamSignal,
216:                     settings.changefeedStream,
217:                   )
218:                 : pumpTransactionEvents(
219:                     response,
220:                     selected.packet,
221:                     (scan, nextSignal) => activeService.nextEvents(scan, nextSignal),
222:                     streamSignal,
223:                     settings.changefeedStream,
224:                   );
225:             // Transfer the initial packet into the pump; do not retain the HTTP
226:             // result/group body for the lifetime of the live stream.
227:             selected = undefined;
228:             await pumping;
229:           } catch (error) {
230:             // Capture before destroy() turns a genuine fault into a disconnect.
231:             if (
232:               response.headersSent &&
233:               !streamSignal.aborted &&
234:               !response.destroyed &&
235:               !firstStreamFault
236:             )
237:               firstStreamFault = { cause: error };
238:             throw error;
239:           } finally {
240:             response.destroy();
241:           }
242:         }, signal);
243:       }
244:     } catch (error) {
245:       if (
246:         signal?.aborted ||
247:         response.headersSent ||
248:         error instanceof ScopeServerOperationAbortedError
249:       ) {
250:         response.destroy();
251:       } else if (error instanceof TransactionStreamCapacityError) {
252:         deliver(response, transactionHttpProblem("temporarily-unavailable", streamContext));
253:       } else if (!response.destroyed) {
254:         response.writeHead(500, {
255:           "content-length": "0",
256:           "cache-control": "private, no-store",
257:           "x-bdp-development": "non-attesting",
258:         });
259:         response.end();
260:       }
261:     }
262:   };
263:   function dispatch(request: IncomingMessage, response: ServerResponse): void {
264:     const task = handle(request, response);
265:     pending.add(task);
266:     void task.then(
267:       () => pending.delete(task),
268:       () => pending.delete(task),
269:     );
270:   }
271:   const server = createServer({ maxHeaderSize: settings.transport.maxHeaderSize }, dispatch);
272:   // Node emits CONNECT separately from ordinary requests. This service never
273:   // opens a tunnel: the same raw target/method gate determines its refusal.
274:   server.on("connect", (request, socket) => {
275:     if (!(socket instanceof Socket)) {
276:       socket.destroy();
277:       return;
278:     }
279:     const response = new ServerResponse(request);
280:     response.assignSocket(socket);
281:     response.setHeader("connection", "close");
282:     response.once("finish", () => socket.end());
283:     response.once("error", () => socket.destroy());
284:     dispatch(request, response);
285:   });
286:   server.requestTimeout = settings.transport.requestTimeout;
287:   server.headersTimeout = settings.transport.headersTimeout;
288:   server.timeout = settings.transport.timeout;
289:   server.keepAliveTimeout = settings.transport.keepAliveTimeout;
290:   for (const key of [
291:     "maxHeaderSize",
292:     "requestTimeout",
293:     "headersTimeout",
294:     "timeout",
295:     "keepAliveTimeout",
296:   ] as const)
297:     if (Reflect.get(server, key) !== settings.transport[key])
298:       throw new Error("runtime transport configuration mismatch");
299:   server.on("timeout", (socket) => socket.destroy());
300:   const closeListener = () =>
301:     new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
302:       server.close((error) => (error ? reject(error) : resolve()));
303:       server.closeAllConnections();
304:     });
305:   let owner: ReturnType<typeof openTransactionStore> | undefined;
306:   try {
307:     await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
308:       server.once("error", reject);
309:       server.listen(captured.port, settings.transport.host, () => {
310:         server.removeListener("error", reject);
311:         resolve();
312:       });
313:     });
314:     const address = server.address();
315:     if (!address || typeof address === "string")
316:       throw new Error("missing private transaction address");
317:     listenUrl = `http://127.0.0.1:${address.port}/`;
318:     scope = configuredScope ?? new URL("acme/", listenUrl).href;
319:     owner = openTransactionStore({
320:       file: path.join(captured.directory, "transaction.sqlite"),
321:       scope,
322:       create: captured.create,
323:       types,
324:       ...(contracts ? { contracts } : {}),
325:       receiptMaximumItems: settings.receiptMaximumItems,
326:       maintenanceIntervalMs: settings.maintenanceIntervalMs,
327:       ...(capturedSeams.clock ? { clock: capturedSeams.clock } : {}),
328:     });
329:     service = createTransactionService(owner, {
330:       ownerLifetime: "service",
331:       maximumStreams: settings.changefeedStream.maximumStreams,
332:       ...(capturedSeams.scheduler ? { scheduler: capturedSeams.scheduler } : {}),
333:       comparison: { budgetMs: settings.comparisonBudgetMs, ...capturedSeams.comparison },
334:       minimumReads: { budgetMs: settings.minimumReadBudgetMs, ...capturedSeams.minimumReads },
335:       read: captured.readOptions(scope),
336:     });
337:     configurationMetadata = captured.metadata(owner.readConfiguration(), scope);
338:     schemas = createTransactionSchemaHttp(scope, contracts, service.schemaObserved);
339:     handler = createTransactionHttpHandler(service, scope, capturedSeams.maximumOperations);
340:   } catch (error) {
341:     const failures: unknown[] = [error];
342:     if (server.listening)
343:       try {
344:         await closeListener();
345:       } catch (failure) {
346:         failures.push(failure);
347:       }
348:     try {
349:       if (service) await service.close();
350:       else owner?.close();
351:     } catch (failure) {
352:       failures.push(failure);
353:     }
354:     throw failures.length === 1
355:       ? error
356:       : new AggregateError(failures, "transaction runtime startup cleanup failed");
357:   }
358:   let completion: Promise<void> | undefined;
359:   return Object.freeze({
360:     scope,
361:     listenUrl,
362:     configurationMetadata,
363:     /** Test-only inventory, not an HTTP Operation Directory. */ targets: handler.targets,
364:     close(): Promise<void> {
365:       if (!completion) {
366:         closing = true;
367:         completion = (async () => {
368:           const failures: unknown[] = [];
369:           try {
370:             await closeListener();
371:           } catch (error) {
372:             failures.push(error);
373:           }
374:           await Promise.allSettled([...pending]);
375:           try {
376:             await service?.close();
377:           } catch (error) {
378:             failures.push(error);
379:           }
380:           if (firstStreamFault) failures.unshift(firstStreamFault.cause);
381:           if (failures.length)
382:             throw new AggregateError(failures, "transaction runtime shutdown failed");
383:         })();
384:         void completion.catch(() => {});
385:       }
386:       return completion;
387:     },
388:   });
389: }

===== ACTUAL FULL SOURCE scripts/transaction-installed-observation.mjs =====
1: /** Bounded inherited-IPC launcher. No request or observation emulation. */
2: import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
3: import { mkdir } from "node:fs/promises";
4: export async function launchInstalledTransaction(entry, configuration, signal) {
5:   signal?.throwIfAborted();
6:   // Explicit launcher-owned provisioning; reopen keeps the directory/create:false.
7:   if (configuration.create) await mkdir(configuration.directory, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
8:   signal?.throwIfAborted();
9:   const child = spawn(process.execPath, [entry], {
10:     cwd: "/tmp",
11:     stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "pipe", "ipc"],
12:   });
13:   let bytes = 0;
14:   for (const stream of [child.stdout, child.stderr])
15:     stream.on("data", (chunk) => {
16:       bytes += chunk.length;
17:       if (bytes > 65536) child.kill("SIGKILL");
18:     });
19:   const exited = new Promise((resolve) =>
20:     child.once("exit", (code, signal) => resolve({ code, signal })),
21:   );
22:   async function bounded(promise) {
23:     let timer;
24:     try {
25:       return await Promise.race([
26:         promise,
27:         new Promise((_, reject) => {
28:           timer = setTimeout(() => {
29:             child.kill("SIGKILL");
30:             reject(new Error("installed transaction process deadline"));
31:           }, 10000);
32:         }),
33:       ]);
34:     } finally {
35:       clearTimeout(timer);
36:     }
37:   }
38:   async function drainFailure(error) {
39:     child.kill("SIGKILL");
40:     try {
41:       await bounded(exited);
42:     } catch (cleanup) {
43:       throw new AggregateError([error, cleanup], "installed transaction cleanup failed");
44:     }
45:     throw error;
46:   }
47:   let closing;
48:   const close = () =>
49:     (closing ??= (async () => {
50:       try {
51:         if (child.connected) child.send({ command: "close" });
52:         const result = await bounded(exited);
53:         if (result.code !== 0 || result.signal !== null)
54:           throw new Error("installed transaction child did not close cleanly");
55:       } catch (error) {
56:         await drainFailure(error);
57:       } finally {
58:         signal?.removeEventListener("abort", onAbort);
59:       }
60:     })());
61:   const onAbort = () => {
62:     void close().catch(() => {});
63:   };
64:   signal?.addEventListener("abort", onAbort, { once: true });
65:   if (signal?.aborted) onAbort();
66:   let detachReadyAbort = () => {};
67:   try {
68:     const ready = await bounded(
69:       new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
70:         const abort = () => reject(signal.reason);
71:         signal?.addEventListener("abort", abort, { once: true });
72:         detachReadyAbort = () => signal?.removeEventListener("abort", abort);
73:         if (signal?.aborted) {
74:           abort();
75:           return;
76:         }
77:         child.once("error", reject);
78:         child.once("message", resolve);
79:         void exited.then(() => reject(new Error("installed transaction exited before ready")));
80:         child.send({ configuration });
81:       }),
82:     );
83:     signal?.throwIfAborted();
84:     if (!ready || ready.event !== "qualification.ready" || JSON.stringify(ready).length > 65536)
85:       throw new Error("invalid installed ready message");
86:     return { child, ready, close };
87:   } catch (error) {
88:     try {
89:       await close();
90:     } catch (cleanup) {
91:       throw new AggregateError([error, cleanup], "installed transaction startup cleanup failed");
92:     }
93:     throw error;
94:   } finally {
95:     detachReadyAbort();
96:   }
97: }

92: export function createTransactionalExecutionBinding(options: {
93:   readonly baseline: ReturnType<typeof installedTransactionBinding>;
94:   readonly sourceCommit: string;
95:   readonly fixtureSource: { bytes: Uint8Array };
96:   readonly exactMode: ExactHttpModeOptions;
97:   readonly launch: (
98:     scenario: string,
99:     reopen: boolean,
100:     signal: AbortSignal,
101:   ) => Promise<InstalledTransactionChild>;
102: }) {
103:   let active:
104:     | (TransactionalSession & { controller: AbortController; pending: Set<Promise<unknown>> })
105:     | undefined;
106:   let generation = 0;
107:   let presentation:
108:     | { scenario: ExecutableScenario; scope: string; seed: number; fixture: ConformanceFixture }
109:     | undefined;
110:   let reopening = false;
111:   const target = createRawHttpScenarioTarget(
112:     async (scenario, scope, seed, fixture, signal) => {
113:       const child = await options.launch(scenario.id, reopening, signal);
114:       try {
115:         if (signal.aborted) throw new Error("installed preparation aborted");
116:         const actual = installedTransactionBinding(
117:           child.ready,
118:           options.sourceCommit,
119:           options.fixtureSource,
120:         );
121:         assertSameServedProfileConfiguration(options.baseline.served, actual.served);
122:         if (
123:           actual.runtimeConfigurationIdentity !== options.baseline.runtimeConfigurationIdentity ||
124:           actual.authorityMode !== options.baseline.authorityMode
125:         )
126:           throw new Error("installed runtime identity mismatch");
127:         const listenUrl = child.ready.listenUrl as string,
128:           address = new URL(listenUrl);
129:         const controller = new AbortController(),
130:           pending = new Set<Promise<unknown>>();
131:         const binding = Object.freeze({
132:           scenario: scenario.id,
133:           generation: ++generation,
134:           listenUrl,
135:           runtimeConfigurationIdentity: actual.runtimeConfigurationIdentity,
136:           responseLimits: Object.freeze({
137:             headerBytes: 65536 as const,
138:             bodyBytes: 1048576 as const,
139:             wireBytes: 2097152 as const,
140:           }),
141:           configurationDigest: createHash("sha256")
142:             .update(JSON.stringify(actual.served))
143:             .digest("hex"),
144:         });
145:         const current = {
146:           binding,
147:           controller,
148:           pending,
149:           signal: AbortSignal.any([controller.signal, signal]),
150:           dialRoute: Object.freeze({
151:             transport: "plain" as const,
152:             host: address.hostname,
153:             port: Number(address.port),
154:           }),
155:           resolveCredentials: options.exactMode.resolveCredentials,
156:           register<T>(operation: Promise<T>) {
157:             pending.add(operation);
158:             void operation.then(
159:               () => pending.delete(operation),
160:               () => pending.delete(operation),
161:             );
162:             return operation;
163:           },
164:         };
165:         active = current;
166:         presentation = { scenario, scope, seed, fixture };
167:         return {
168:           capabilities: fixture.capabilities,
169:           bindings: fixture.bindings,
170:           dialRoute: current.dialRoute,
171:           async close() {
172:             if (active === current) active = undefined;
173:             controller.abort();
174:             await Promise.allSettled([...pending]);
175:             await child.close();
176:           },
177:         };
178:       } catch (error) {
179:         try {
180:           await child.close();
181:         } catch (cleanup) {
182:           throw new AggregateError([error, cleanup], "installed session setup cleanup failed");
183:         }
184:         throw error;
185:       }
186:     },
187:     {
188:       exactMode: options.exactMode,
189:       requestTimeoutMs: 5000,
190:       cleanupTimeoutMs: 10000,
191:       maximumHeaderBytes: 65536,
192:       maximumBodyBytes: 1048576,
193:     },
194:   );
195:   const context: TransactionalRunContext = {
196:     current(scenario) {
197:       if (!active || active.binding.scenario !== scenario || active.signal.aborted)
198:         throw new Error("installed session unavailable");
199:       return active;
200:     },
201:     async reopen(scenario, signal) {
202:       const old = presentation;
203:       if (!old || old.scenario.id !== scenario || !active)
204:         throw new Error("reopen outside active scenario");
205:       await target.close(signal);
206:       reopening = true;
207:       try {
208:         await target.harness.prepare(old.scenario, old.scope, old.seed, old.fixture, signal);
209:       } finally {
210:         reopening = false;
211:       }
212:     },
213:   };
214:   return Object.freeze({ target, context });
215: }

===== ACTUAL FULL SOURCE packages/server/src/transaction-consistency.ts =====
1: /** Source-private canonical minimum-read subset. No continuation or malformed-
2:  * tuple protocol ruling; those categories remain explicit development gaps. */
3: import { setTimeout as delay } from "node:timers/promises";
4: import { isProxy } from "node:util/types";
5: import { ScopeServerOperationAbortedError } from "./read-request.js";
6: import type { TransactionReadContext } from "./transaction-store.js";
7: 
8: export interface TransactionMinimum {
9:   readonly scopeEpoch: string;
10:   readonly authorizationView: string;
11:   readonly minimumPosition: string;
12: }
13: const token = /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,256}$/;
14: const position = (value: unknown): number | undefined => {
15:   if (value === "genesis") return 0;
16:   if (typeof value !== "string" || !/^pos-(0|[1-9][0-9]*)$/.test(value)) return undefined;
17:   const number = Number(value.slice(4));
18:   return Number.isSafeInteger(number) ? number : undefined;
19: };
20: export function snapshotTransactionMinimum(value: unknown): TransactionMinimum {
21:   if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || isProxy(value))
22:     throw new TypeError("unsupported minimum tuple");
23:   const fields = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptors(value);
24:   if (
25:     Object.keys(fields).sort().join(",") !== "authorizationView,minimumPosition,scopeEpoch" ||
26:     Reflect.ownKeys(value).length !== 3 ||
27:     Object.values(fields).some((field) => !("value" in field))
28:   )
29:     throw new TypeError("unsupported minimum tuple");
30:   const scopeEpoch: unknown = fields.scopeEpoch?.value;
31:   const authorizationView: unknown = fields.authorizationView?.value;
32:   const minimumPosition: unknown = fields.minimumPosition?.value;
33:   if (
34:     typeof scopeEpoch !== "string" ||
35:     !token.test(scopeEpoch) ||
36:     typeof authorizationView !== "string" ||
37:     !token.test(authorizationView) ||
38:     typeof minimumPosition !== "string" ||
39:     !token.test(minimumPosition) ||
40:     position(minimumPosition) === undefined
41:   )
42:     throw new TypeError("unsupported minimum tuple");
43:   return Object.freeze({ scopeEpoch, authorizationView, minimumPosition });
44: }
45: export function transactionMinimumHeaders(headers: Headers): TransactionMinimum | undefined {
46:   try {
47:     return snapshotTransactionMinimum({
48:       scopeEpoch: headers.get("bdp-scope-epoch"),
49:       authorizationView: headers.get("bdp-authorization-view"),
50:       minimumPosition: headers.get("bdp-minimum-scope-position"),
51:     });
52:   } catch {
53:     return undefined;
54:   }
55: }
56: export class TransactionMinimumFailure extends Error {
57:   constructor(
58:     readonly reason: "behind" | "foreign-view" | "cursor-expired" | "catch-up-timeout",
59:     readonly context: TransactionReadContext,
60:   ) {
61:     super(`transaction minimum read: ${reason}`);
62:   }
63: }
64: /** Runs within the representation's owner entry, after authorization. */
65: export function checkTransactionMinimum(
66:   minimum: TransactionMinimum,
67:   context: TransactionReadContext,
68: ): void {
69:   if (minimum.scopeEpoch !== context.scopeEpoch)
70:     throw new TransactionMinimumFailure("cursor-expired", context);
71:   if (minimum.authorizationView !== context.authorizationView)
72:     throw new TransactionMinimumFailure("foreign-view", context);
73:   const required = position(minimum.minimumPosition),
74:     current = position(context.scopePosition);
75:   if (required === undefined || current === undefined)
76:     throw new Error("invalid owner minimum position");
77:   if (current < required) throw new TransactionMinimumFailure("behind", context);
78: }
79: export interface TransactionMinimumWaitOptions {
80:   readonly budgetMs?: number;
81:   readonly now?: () => number;
82:   readonly wait?: (milliseconds: number) => Promise<void>;
83: }
84: /** No SQLite entry survives an await. The original finite deadline never renews. */
85: export async function waitForTransactionMinimum<T>(
86:   attempt: () => T | Promise<T>,
87:   options: TransactionMinimumWaitOptions = {},
88:   signal?: AbortSignal,
89: ): Promise<T> {
90:   const budget = options.budgetMs ?? 1000,
91:     now = options.now ?? (() => performance.now()),
92:     wait = options.wait ?? ((milliseconds) => delay(milliseconds));
93:   if (
94:     !Number.isSafeInteger(budget) ||
95:     budget <= 0 ||
96:     budget > 2147483647 ||
97:     typeof now !== "function" ||
98:     typeof wait !== "function"
99:   )
100:     throw new TypeError("finite minimum-read wait policy required");
101:   const started = now();
102:   if (!Number.isFinite(started)) throw new TypeError("invalid minimum-read clock");
103:   const deadline = started + budget;
104:   let previous = started;
105:   if (!Number.isFinite(deadline)) throw new TypeError("invalid minimum-read deadline");
106:   for (;;) {
107:     if (signal?.aborted) throw new ScopeServerOperationAbortedError();
108:     try {
109:       return await attempt();
110:     } catch (error) {
111:       if (!(error instanceof TransactionMinimumFailure) || error.reason !== "behind") throw error;
112:       const current = now();
113:       if (!Number.isFinite(current) || current < previous)
114:         throw new TypeError("invalid minimum-read clock");
115:       previous = current;
116:       if (current >= deadline)
117:         throw new TransactionMinimumFailure("catch-up-timeout", error.context);
118:       await wait(Math.min(5, deadline - current));
119:     }
120:   }
121: }

===== ACTUAL FULL SOURCE packages/server/src/transaction-sse.ts =====
1: /** Source-private one-frame-at-a-time pump for change groups and Events. */
2: import { constants } from "node:buffer";
3: import { setTimeout as delay, setImmediate as yieldTurn } from "node:timers/promises";
4: import { stringifyJsonValue } from "@bdp/protocol";
5: import {
6:   TRANSACTION_CHANGEFEED_BYTES,
7:   type TransactionChangefeedPage,
8: } from "./transaction-changefeed.js";
9: 
10: import { TRANSACTION_EVENT_STREAM } from "./transaction-events.js";
11: import type { TransactionEvent } from "./transaction-batch.js";
12: import type { TransactionEventPacket, TransactionEventScan } from "./transaction-store.js";
13: 
14: export const TRANSACTION_SSE_DEFAULTS = Object.freeze({
15:   pollMilliseconds: 100,
16:   heartbeatMilliseconds: 5000,
17:   maximumStreams: 1,
18: });
19: /** Node timers clamp invalid/out-of-range values; refuse rather than busy-poll. */
20: export function validateTransactionStreamTimers(
21:   settings: { readonly pollMilliseconds: number; readonly heartbeatMilliseconds: number },
22:   idleTimeout?: number,
23: ): void {
24:   for (const value of [settings.pollMilliseconds, settings.heartbeatMilliseconds])
25:     if (!Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value <= 0 || value > 2_147_483_647)
26:       throw new TypeError("invalid transaction stream timer");
27:   if (idleTimeout !== undefined && settings.heartbeatMilliseconds >= idleTimeout)
28:     throw new TypeError("stream heartbeat must precede socket idle timeout");
29: }
30: // Includes id/event/data framing beyond the already bounded finite page. This
31: // is a source-derived proof, never a new post-commit rejection of a legal group.
32: export const TRANSACTION_SSE_FRAME_BYTES = TRANSACTION_CHANGEFEED_BYTES + 512;
33: export const TRANSACTION_EVENT_FRAME_BYTES = TRANSACTION_EVENT_STREAM.packetBytes + 512;
34: if (TRANSACTION_EVENT_FRAME_BYTES >= constants.MAX_STRING_LENGTH)
35:   throw new Error("pinned runtime cannot encode the bounded Event frame");
36: if (TRANSACTION_SSE_FRAME_BYTES >= constants.MAX_STRING_LENGTH)
37:   throw new Error("pinned runtime cannot encode the bounded changefeed frame");
38: 
39: export function transactionChangeFrame(group: TransactionChangefeedPage["groups"][number]): string {
40:   return `id: ${group.checkpoint}\nevent: change-group\ndata: ${stringifyJsonValue(group)}\n\n`;
41: }
42: 
43: export function transactionEventFrame(event: TransactionEvent): string {
44:   return `id: ${event.id}\nevent: ${event.type}\ndata: ${stringifyJsonValue(event)}\n\n`;
45: }
46: 
47: interface Sink {
48:   readonly destroyed: boolean;
49:   write(value: string): boolean;
50:   once(event: "drain" | "close", listener: () => void): unknown;
51:   once(event: "error", listener: (error: Error) => void): unknown;
52:   removeListener(
53:     event: "drain" | "error" | "close",
54:     listener: (() => void) | ((error: Error) => void),
55:   ): unknown;
56: }
57: /** write(false) accepted this frame into Node, but permits no subsequent owner
58:  * read/comment until drain. It is not a client replica acknowledgement. */
59: async function write(sink: Sink, value: string, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
60:   signal.throwIfAborted();
61:   if (sink.destroyed) throw new Error("stream transport closed");
62:   if (sink.write(value)) return;
63:   await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
64:     const clean = () => {
65:       sink.removeListener("drain", drained);
66:       sink.removeListener("error", failed);
67:       sink.removeListener("close", closed);
68:       signal.removeEventListener("abort", aborted);
69:     };
70:     const drained = () => {
71:       clean();
72:       resolve();
73:     };
74:     const failed = (error: unknown) => {
75:       clean();
76:       reject(error);
77:     };
78:     const closed = () => failed(new Error("stream transport closed"));
79:     const aborted = () => failed(signal.reason);
80:     sink.once("drain", drained);
81:     sink.once("error", failed);
82:     sink.once("close", closed);
83:     signal.addEventListener("abort", aborted, { once: true });
84:     if (signal.aborted) aborted();
85:     else if (sink.destroyed) closed();
86:   });
87: }
88: 
89: interface Packet<Item, Cursor> {
90:   readonly items: readonly Item[];
91:   readonly continuation: Cursor;
92:   readonly caughtUp: boolean;
93: }
94: type Timers = { readonly pollMilliseconds: number; readonly heartbeatMilliseconds: number };
95: /** One private delivery loop. It retains only the current packet; continuations
96:  * advance after its final drain, never on a speculative owner pull. */
97: async function pump<Item, Cursor>(
98:   sink: Sink,
99:   initial: Packet<Item, Cursor> | undefined,
100:   next: (cursor: Cursor, signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<Packet<Item, Cursor>>,
101:   frame: (item: Item) => string,
102:   signal: AbortSignal,
103:   settings: Timers,
104: ): Promise<void> {
105:   validateTransactionStreamTimers(settings);
106:   let packet = initial;
107:   initial = undefined;
108:   let lastWrite = performance.now();
109:   while (packet && !signal.aborted) {
110:     for (const item of packet.items) {
111:       await write(sink, frame(item), signal);
112:       lastWrite = performance.now();
113:     }
114:     const continuation = packet.continuation,
115:       caughtUp = packet.caughtUp;
116:     packet = undefined;
117:     // Empty historical packets must keep this check active too. Comments obey
118:     // the same write/drain barrier; no timer queues independent writes.
119:     if (performance.now() - lastWrite >= settings.heartbeatMilliseconds) {
120:       await write(sink, ": heartbeat\n\n", signal);
121:       lastWrite = performance.now();
122:     }
123:     if (caughtUp) await delay(settings.pollMilliseconds, undefined, { signal });
124:     else await yieldTurn(undefined, { signal });
125:     signal.throwIfAborted();
126:     packet = await next(continuation, signal);
127:   }
128: }
129: function changePacket(page: TransactionChangefeedPage) {
130:   return {
131:     items: page.groups,
132:     continuation: page.groups.at(-1)?.checkpoint ?? page.after,
133:     caughtUp: page.groups.length === 0,
134:   };
135: }
136: function eventPacket(packet: TransactionEventPacket) {
137:   return { items: packet.events, continuation: packet.scan, caughtUp: packet.caughtUp };
138: }
139: export function pumpTransactionChanges(
140:   sink: Sink,
141:   initial: TransactionChangefeedPage,
142:   next: (after: string, signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<TransactionChangefeedPage>,
143:   signal: AbortSignal,
144:   settings: Timers = TRANSACTION_SSE_DEFAULTS,
145: ): Promise<void> {
146:   return pump(
147:     sink,
148:     changePacket(initial),
149:     async (after, signal) => changePacket(await next(after, signal)),
150:     transactionChangeFrame,
151:     signal,
152:     settings,
153:   );
154: }
155: export function pumpTransactionEvents(
156:   sink: Sink,
157:   initial: TransactionEventPacket,
158:   next: (scan: TransactionEventScan, signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<TransactionEventPacket>,
159:   signal: AbortSignal,
160:   settings: Timers = TRANSACTION_SSE_DEFAULTS,
161: ): Promise<void> {
162:   return pump(
163:     sink,
164:     eventPacket(initial),
165:     async (scan, signal) => eventPacket(await next(scan, signal)),
166:     transactionEventFrame,
167:     signal,
168:     settings,
169:   );
170: }

1: import { existsSync, readdirSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs";
2: import path from "node:path";
3: import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
4: 
5: import { defineConfig } from "vitest/config";
6: 
7: const workspaceRoot = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
8: 
9: /**
10:  * Aliases every `@bdp/*` library package to its TypeScript source so `pnpm
11:  * test` works from a clean checkout without a prior build. A package may also
12:  * own a source-only `test-support/testing.ts` subpath for cross-package integration tests;
13:  * it is deliberately absent from package exports and installed consumers.
14:  * Each package's production `exports` map still points at `dist/index.js`.
15:  *
16:  * Only `packages/*` are scanned: those are the export-bearing libraries that
17:  * tests import as `@bdp/*`. `apps/*` are CLI binaries with no `exports` and a
18:  * `src/main.ts` entrypoint, so aliasing them would register a target that
19:  * does not exist and mask a misconfigured import. The manifest is read
20:  * directly — a missing or malformed `package.json` fails the Vitest config
21:  * with the offending path instead of being silently skipped.
22:  */
23: function collectWorkspaceSourceAliases(): Record<string, string> {
24:   const aliases: Record<string, string> = {};
25:   const packagesDir = path.join(workspaceRoot, "packages");
26:   for (const entry of readdirSync(packagesDir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
27:     if (!entry.isDirectory()) continue;
28:     const packageDir = path.join(packagesDir, entry.name);
29:     const manifestPath = path.join(packageDir, "package.json");
30:     let manifest: unknown;
31:     try {
32:       manifest = JSON.parse(readFileSync(manifestPath, "utf8"));
33:     } catch (error) {
34:       throw new Error(`${manifestPath}: failed to read or parse package.json`, {
35:         cause: error,
36:       });
37:     }
38:     if (!isPlainObject(manifest) || typeof manifest.name !== "string") {
39:       throw new Error(`${manifestPath}: package.json must define a string "name"`);
40:     }
41:     const name = manifest.name;
42:     if (!name.startsWith("@bdp/")) continue;
43:     const sourceEntry = path.join(packageDir, "src", "index.ts");
44:     if (!existsSync(sourceEntry)) {
45:       throw new Error(
46:         `${manifestPath}: workspace package ${name} is missing its source entrypoint at ${sourceEntry}`,
47:       );
48:     }
49:     const testingEntry = path.join(packageDir, "test-support", "testing.ts");
50:     if (existsSync(testingEntry)) aliases[`${name}/testing`] = testingEntry;
51:     if (name === "@bdp/server")
52:       aliases[`${name}/development`] = path.join(packageDir, "src/development.ts");
53:     aliases[name] = sourceEntry;
54:   }
55:   return aliases;
56: }
57: 
58: function isPlainObject(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
59:   return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
60: }
61: 
62: export default defineConfig({
63:   resolve: { alias: collectWorkspaceSourceAliases() },
64:   test: {
65:     coverage: { enabled: false },
66:     include: ["packages/**/*.test.ts", "apps/**/*.test.ts", "scripts/**/*.test.mjs"],
67:     passWithNoTests: false,
68:     // Install the non-emitted admission mock before cross-package test modules
69:     // can instantiate @bdp/server through an adapter dependency.
70:     setupFiles: ["./packages/server/test-support/read-conformance-mock.ts"],
71:   },
72: });

348:   function enter<T>(run: () => T): T {
349:     if (ownerFailure) throw ownerFailure;
350:     if (maintenanceFailure)
351:       throw new Error("transaction maintenance failed; owner fenced", maintenanceFailure);
352:     if (closed || busy) throw new Error("transaction store closed or reentrant");
353:     busy = true;
354:     try {
355:       return run();
356:     } finally {
357:       busy = false;

360:   function fence(cause: unknown): Error {
361:     clearInterval(maintenanceTimer);
362:     // Cleanup can add the original application error to an earlier storage
363:     // failure. Replace the wrapper; mutating its cause would create a cycle.
364:     ownerFailure = new Error("transaction storage failure; owner fenced; reopen before use", {
365:       cause,
366:     });
367:     return ownerFailure;
368:   }
369:   function atomic<T>(run: () => T): T {
370:     if (ownerFailure) throw ownerFailure;
371:     let committing = false;
372:     try {
373:       db.exec("BEGIN IMMEDIATE");
374:       const value = run();
375:       committing = true;
376:       db.exec("COMMIT");
377:       return value;
378:     } catch (error) {
379:       let cleanRollback = false;
380:       let cause: unknown = error;
381:       try {
382:         if (db.isTransaction) {
383:           db.exec("ROLLBACK");
384:           cleanRollback = !db.isTransaction;
385:         }
386:       } catch (rollback) {
387:         cause = new AggregateError([error, rollback], "transaction and rollback failed");
388:       }
389:       const sqlite =
390:         error instanceof Error && "code" in error && String(error.code).startsWith("ERR_SQLITE");
391:       const constraint =
392:         sqlite &&
393:         "errcode" in error &&
394:         typeof error.errcode === "number" &&
395:         Number.isInteger(error.errcode) &&
396:         (error.errcode & 0xff) === 19;
397:       // Even a COMMIT that throws after taking effect has an uncertain outcome.
398:       // Reopen determines its durable state; compensation must not guess at it.
399:       if (committing || !cleanRollback || (sqlite && !constraint)) throw fence(cause);
400:       throw error;
401:     }
402:   }
403:   function cleanupThenThrow(error: unknown, cleanup: () => void): never {
404:     if (ownerFailure) throw error;
405:     try {
406:       cleanup();
407:     } catch (failure) {
408:       const combined = new AggregateError([error, failure], "transaction and cleanup failed");
409:       fence(combined);

601:    * reader remains inside its synchronous entry; no SQLite capability escapes. */
602:   function retainedGroupReader() {
603:     const rowAt = db.prepare(
604:       "SELECT position,length(CAST(body AS BLOB)) AS bytes FROM groups WHERE position>=? AND position<=? ORDER BY position LIMIT 1",
605:     );
606:     const bodyAt = db.prepare("SELECT body FROM groups WHERE position=?");
607:     return (at: number, head: number): { position: number; value: unknown } | undefined => {
608:       const row = rowAt.get(at, head);
609:       if (!row) return undefined;
610:       const position = Number(row.position),
611:         bytes = Number(row.bytes);
612:       if (!Number.isSafeInteger(position) || position < at)
613:         throw new Error("invalid Event group position");
614:       if (!Number.isSafeInteger(bytes) || bytes < 2 || bytes > TRANSACTION_EVENT_GROUP_BYTES)
615:         throw new Error("retained Event group exceeds admitted byte bound");
616:       const body = bodyAt.get(position)?.body;
617:       if (typeof body !== "string") throw new Error("invalid retained Event group body");
618:       return { position, value: JSON.parse(body) as unknown };
619:     };
620:   }

1717:         // Under this no-pruning owner every positive position through head was
1718:         // committed. Its absent row is corruption, not a never-issued cursor.
1719:         if (start > 0 && !db.prepare("SELECT position FROM groups WHERE position=?").get(start))
1720:           throw new Error("missing retained change group");
1721:         if (minimum !== undefined) checkTransactionMinimum(minimum, context);
1722:         const row = retainedGroupReader()(start + 1, head);
1723:         if ((!row && start < head) || (row && row.position !== start + 1))
1724:           throw new Error("missing retained change group");
1725:         const group = row
1726:           ? projectTransactionChangeGroup(row.value, scope, context, row.position)
1727:           : undefined;
1728:         let next: string | null = null;
1729:         if (group && row && row.position < head) {
1730:           if (!db.prepare("SELECT position FROM groups WHERE position=?").get(row.position + 1))
1731:             throw new Error("missing retained change group");
1732:           const url = new URL("changes/", scope);
1733:           url.searchParams.set("after", group.checkpoint);
1734:           next = url.href;
1735:         }
1736:         return Object.freeze({
1737:           kind: "page" as const,
1738:           context,
1739:           page: freezeChangefeedPage({
1740:             scope,
1741:             scopeEpoch: context.scopeEpoch,
1742:             authorizationView: context.authorizationView,
1743:             after,
1744:             observedHeadPosition: context.scopePosition,
1745:             groups: group ? [group] : [],
1746:             next,
1747:           }),
1748:         });
1749:       });
1750:     },
1751:     readConfiguration: () => enter(() => readConfiguration),
1752:     comparisonEpoch: () => {
1753:       if (closed && !ownerFailure && !maintenanceFailure) return undefined;
1754:       return enter(() => metadata("epoch"));
1755:     },
1756:     /** Private deadline observation: one owner entry and one atomic metadata read. */
1757:     observeComparisonDeadline(
1758:       expectedEpoch: string,
1759:     ):
1760:       | { readonly kind: "observed"; readonly context: TransactionReadContext }
1761:       | { readonly kind: "owner-stopped" } {
1762:       if (closed && !ownerFailure && !maintenanceFailure)
1763:         return Object.freeze({ kind: "owner-stopped" });
1764:       return enter(() =>
1765:         atomic(() => {
1766:           if (metadata("epoch") !== expectedEpoch)
1767:             return Object.freeze({ kind: "owner-stopped" as const });
1768:           return Object.freeze({ kind: "observed" as const, context: readContext() });
1769:         }),
1770:       );

71: export function createTransactionService(owner: Owner, options: TransactionServiceOptions) {
72:   if (options.ownerLifetime !== "service")
73:     throw new TypeError("explicit service owner lifetime required");
74:   const scope = owner.scope;
75:   const supplied = options.read,
76:     limits = supplied.advertisedLimits,
77:     page = limits.page,
78:     selector = limits.selector,
79:     enforcedSelector = supplied.selectorLimits;
80:   const suppliedPagination = supplied.pagination;
81:   const pagination = { ...suppliedPagination };
82:   // Capture callback values once, but keep the receiver the Read plane used
83:   // before scalar snapshotting. Stateful callbacks belong to their caller,
84:   // not to the frozen copy. Leave invalid values for existing validation.
85:   const clock = pagination.clock,
86:     generateOpaqueToken = pagination.generateOpaqueToken;
87:   if (typeof clock === "function") pagination.clock = clock.bind(suppliedPagination);
88:   if (typeof generateOpaqueToken === "function")
89:     pagination.generateOpaqueToken = generateOpaqueToken.bind(suppliedPagination);
90:   const capturedRead: TransactionReadOptions = Object.freeze({
91:     selectorLimits: Object.freeze({
92:       bytes: enforcedSelector.bytes,
93:       depth: enforcedSelector.depth,
94:       nodes: enforcedSelector.nodes,
95:     }),
96:     pagination: Object.freeze(pagination),
97:     advertisedLimits: Object.freeze({
98:       page: Object.freeze({ defaultItems: page.defaultItems, maximumItems: page.maximumItems }),
99:       selector: Object.freeze({
100:         bytes: selector.bytes,
101:         depth: selector.depth,
102:         nodes: selector.nodes,
103:       }),
104:       cursorTtlMilliseconds: limits.cursorTtlMilliseconds,
105:     }),
106:   });
107:   const maximum = owner.readConfiguration().receiptMaximumItems;
108:   if (
109:     capturedRead.advertisedLimits.page.maximumItems !== maximum ||
110:     capturedRead.pagination.maxPageItems !== maximum
111:   )
112:     throw new TypeError("receipt page maximum differs from installed owner");
113:   const scheduler = options.scheduler ?? createNodeLifecycleScheduler();
114:   const turn = scheduler.turn.bind(scheduler);
115:   const comparison = Object.freeze({ ...options.comparison });
116:   const minimumReads = Object.freeze({ ...options.minimumReads });
117:   const read = createTransactionReadPlane(owner, capturedRead);
118:   const active = new Set<Promise<unknown>>();
119:   const maximumStreams = options.maximumStreams ?? 1;
120:   if (!Number.isSafeInteger(maximumStreams) || maximumStreams < 1)
121:     throw new TypeError("invalid private stream capacity");
122:   const streams = new Map<AbortController, Promise<void>>();
123:   let accepting = true;
124:   let cleanupFailure: AggregateError | undefined;
125:   let closing: Promise<void> | undefined;
126:   const completion = Promise.withResolvers<void>();
127:   void completion.promise.catch(() => {});
128:   function check() {
129:     if (cleanupFailure)
130:       throw new Error("transaction service cleanup failed; close and reopen", {
131:         cause: cleanupFailure,
132:       });
133:     if (!accepting) throw new Error("transaction service is closing or closed");
134:   }
135:   function run<T>(start: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
136:     check();
137:     const task = Promise.withResolvers<T>();
138:     active.add(task.promise);
139:     // Observe rejected detached delivery without changing what its caller sees.
140:     void task.promise.then(
141:       () => active.delete(task.promise),
142:       () => active.delete(task.promise),
143:     );
144:     try {
145:       task.resolve(start());
146:     } catch (error) {
147:       task.reject(error);
148:     }
149:     return task.promise;
150:   }
151:   function nextTurn(): Promise<void> {
152:     return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
153:       let scheduling = true,
154:         inline = false,
155:         live = true;
156:       let cancel: (() => void) | undefined;
157:       try {
158:         cancel = turn(() => {
159:           if (!live) return;
160:           if (scheduling) {
161:             inline = true;
162:             return;
163:           }
164:           live = false;
165:           resolve();
166:         });
167:         scheduling = false;
168:         if (typeof cancel !== "function") throw new TypeError("scheduler must return cancellation");
169:         if (inline) throw new TypeError("scheduler invoked callback inline");
170:       } catch (error) {
171:         live = false;
172:         try {
173:           if (typeof cancel === "function") cancel();
174:         } catch (cleanup) {
175:           reject(
176:             new AggregateError([error, cleanup], "transaction scheduling and cancellation failed"),
177:           );
178:           return;
179:         }
180:         reject(error);
181:       }
182:     });
183:   }
184:   function checked(observed: {
185:     readonly receipt: TransactionReceipt;
186:     readonly context: TransactionReadContext;
187:   }): ReceiptObservation {
188:     const results = observed.receipt.results;
189:     // URL prefixes are construction-guaranteed by this store; retain these
190:     // checks as defense in depth alongside the independent result bounds.
191:     if (
192:       observed.receipt.status === "completed" &&
193:       observed.receipt.detail === "available" &&
194:       (!Array.isArray(results) ||
195:         results.length === 0 ||
196:         results.length > capturedRead.advertisedLimits.page.maximumItems ||
197:         (observed.receipt.next !== null &&
198:           (typeof observed.receipt.next !== "string" ||
199:             !observed.receipt.next.startsWith(`${observed.receipt.id}/pages/`) ||
200:             results.length !== maximum)))
201:     )
202:       throw new Error("transaction receipt exceeds supported inline result bound", {
203:         cause: { id: observed.receipt.id, key: observed.receipt.idempotencyKey },
204:       });
205:     return Object.freeze({ kind: "receipt", ...observed });
206:   }

208:     principal: string,
209:     id: string,
210:     minimum?: TransactionMinimum,

18: export const TRANSACTION_EVENT_PAGE_BYTES = 4 * 1024 * 1024;
19: /** Actual private stream mechanics, not public paging limits. */
20: export const TRANSACTION_EVENT_STREAM = Object.freeze({
21:   maximumGroupsPerObservation: 1,
22:   packetBytes: TRANSACTION_EVENT_PAGE_BYTES,
23: });
24: // Same closed-shape proof as the admitted tuple in the implementation progress
25: // document. This bounds encoded group bytes, not parsed object/heap overhead.
26: const R = TRANSACTION_RUNTIME_LIMITS.representationBytes,
27:   Q = TRANSACTION_RUNTIME_LIMITS.requestBodyBytes,
28:   S = TRANSACTION_RUNTIME_LIMITS.scopeSourceBytes,
29:   N = 7 * Q,
30:   A = 8192,
31:   H = 1048576,
32:   K = TRANSACTION_RUNTIME_LIMITS.matchedResources + TRANSACTION_RUNTIME_LIMITS.maximumOperations;
33: export const TRANSACTION_EVENT_GROUP_BYTES = Math.min(
34:   TRANSACTION_RUNTIME_LIMITS.inducedEvents * (2.5 * R + N + S) + K * A + H,
35:   K * (Math.max(9 * R + 4 * S, 5 * R + 2 * N + 2 * S) + A) + H,
36: );
37: // Largest owned update <= 2R+N+source+A. A Resource source is at most R
38: // plus its view suffix; the page repeats it in source and next. Reserve 1024
39: // extra bytes for suffixes, cursor and envelope. Scope source is smaller.
40: export const TRANSACTION_EVENT_FIRST_PAGE_BYTES = 5 * R + N + A + 1024;
41: if (TRANSACTION_EVENT_FIRST_PAGE_BYTES > TRANSACTION_EVENT_PAGE_BYTES)
42:   throw new Error("transaction tuple cannot support the finite Event page budget");

535:   const definition = readProblemDefinitionFor(code);

537:     type: definition.type,

540:     status: definition.status,
541:     // The caller still needs positive incomplete-version evidence. This default
542:     // honestly makes no assertion about the inventory of missing locations.
543:     ...(code === "revision-unretained" ? { missing: { complete: false, items: [] } } : {}),
544:     ...(detail === undefined ? {} : { detail }),
545:   };
546: }

76:   ) => Readonly<Record<string, string>>;

551: /** Does not admit a profile: validates the full Scope/route preconditions, even standalone. */
552: export function validateExactRunConfiguration(
553:   mode: Pick<ExactHttpModeOptions, "scope" | "routes">,
554:   url: string,
555:   method: HttpMethod,
556: ): void {
557:   try {
558:     const fields = dataRecord(mode);
559:     const { scope, routes } = snapshotExactRoutes(fields.scope, fields.routes);
560:     const target = canonicalHttpUrl(url);
561:     if (
562:       !isHttpMethod(method) ||
563:       target.origin !== scope.origin ||
564:       !target.pathname.startsWith(scope.pathname) ||
565:       !routes.some((route) => route.url === target.href && route.method === method)
566:     )
567:       configuration();
568:   } catch {
569:     configuration();
570:   }
571: }

573: function copyBoundedBytes(value: unknown, maximum: number): Buffer {
574:   if (!(value instanceof Uint8Array) || BYTE_LENGTH === undefined) return configuration();
575:   const length: number = Reflect.apply(BYTE_LENGTH, value, []);
576:   if (
577:     length > maximum ||
578:     BYTE_BUFFER === undefined ||
579:     Reflect.apply(BYTE_BUFFER, value, []) instanceof SharedArrayBuffer
580:   )
581:     return configuration();
582:   const copied = Buffer.alloc(length);
583:   Reflect.apply(SET_BYTES, copied, [value]);
584:   return copied;
585: }

587: function requestDeadline(signal: AbortSignal, deadline: number): number {
588:   if (signal.aborted) throw new HttpTransportError("abort", "HTTP exchange was aborted");
589:   const remaining = deadline - performance.now();
590:   if (remaining <= 0) throw new HttpTransportError("timeout", "HTTP exchange timed out");
591:   return Math.max(1, Math.ceil(remaining));
592: }

594: function safeCredentialTarget(target: Buffer, semantic: URL): boolean {
595:   const text = target.toString("latin1");
596:   if (/[^\x21-\x7e]/.test(text) || text.includes("\\")) return false;
597:   if (text.startsWith("/") && !text.startsWith("//")) return true;
598:   try {
599:     const url = new URL(text, semantic.origin);
600:     return (
601:       url.origin === semantic.origin &&
602:       url.username === "" &&
603:       url.password === "" &&
604:       (text.startsWith(`${semantic.protocol}//`) || text.startsWith("//"))
605:     );
606:   } catch {
607:     return false;
608:   }
609: }

611: async function executeExact(
612:   input: ExactHttpExchangeRequest,
613:   route: RawHttpDialRoute,
614:   mode: ExactHttpModeOptions | undefined,
615:   timeoutMs: number,
616:   maximumHeaderBytes: number,
617:   maximumBodyBytes: number,
618: ): Promise<HttpExchangeResponse> {

620:   let state: RequestWriteState = "not-started";

628:       "credentialRef",

639:       url.pathname.length + url.search.length > mode.maximumRequestHeaderBytes

647:               request.rawRequestTarget,
648:               Math.min(MAXIMUM_RAW_REQUEST_TARGET_BYTES, mode.maximumRequestHeaderBytes),
649:             ),
650:             url,
651:           );

653:       raw.bodyBytes === undefined
654:         ? undefined
655:         : copyBoundedBytes(raw.bodyBytes, mode.maximumRequestBodyBytes);
656:     if (mode.profile === "read" && body !== undefined) return configuration();

659:     const append = (name: string, value: string): void => {
660:       headBytes += name.length + value.length + 4;
661:       if (headBytes > mode.maximumRequestHeaderBytes) configuration();
662:       lines.push(Object.freeze({ name, value }));
663:     };
664:     const sourceLines = arrayItems(raw.headerLines);
665:     for (let i = 0; i < sourceLines.length; i++) {

667:       if (
668:         typeof entry.name !== "string" ||
669:         typeof entry.value !== "string" ||

671:         !HTTP_FIELD_VALUE.test(entry.value)

673:         return configuration();

675:       if (isSecretHeaderName(name) || RESERVED_FIELDS.has(name)) return configuration();
676:       append(entry.name, entry.value);
677:     }
678:     if (headBytes > mode.maximumRequestHeaderBytes) return configuration();
679:     validateExactRunConfiguration(mode, url.href, request.method);
680:     const ref = request.credentialRef ?? mode.defaultCredentialRef;
681:     if (typeof ref !== "string" || !HANDLE_ID.test(ref) || ref.length > 128) return configuration();
682:     const handle = mode.credentialHandles.find(({ id }) => id === ref);
683:     if (
684:       handle === undefined ||
685:       (handle.headerNames.length > 0 && !safeCredentialTarget(target, url))
686:     )
687:       return configuration();
688:     requestDeadline(signal, deadline);
689:     const credentials = dataRecord(
690:       mode.resolveCredentials(
691:         Object.freeze({
692:           scope: mode.scope,
693:           url: url.href,
694:           method: request.method,
695:           credentialRef: ref,
696:           signal,
697:         }),
698:       ),
699:     );
700:     requestDeadline(signal, deadline);
701:     const seen = new Set<string>();
702:     for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(credentials)) {
703:       const normalized = name.toLowerCase();
704:       if (
705:         !handle.headerNames.includes(normalized) ||
706:         seen.has(normalized) ||
707:         typeof value !== "string" ||
708:         !HTTP_FIELD_VALUE.test(value) ||
709:         !HTTP_FIELD_NAME.test(name)
710:       )
711:         return configuration();
712:       seen.add(normalized);
713:       append(name, value);
714:     }
715:     if (seen.size !== handle.headerNames.length) return configuration();
716:     for (const [name, value] of [
717:       ["Accept", "*/*"],
718:       ["Accept-Language", "*"],
719:       ["Accept-Encoding", "identity"],
720:       ["User-Agent", "bdp-conformance/0"],
721:     ] as const)
722:       if (!lines.some((line) => line.name.toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase()))
723:         append(name, value);
724:     append("Host", url.host);
725:     append("Connection", "close");
726:     if (body !== undefined) append("Content-Length", String(body.length));
727:     const head = Buffer.concat([
728:       Buffer.from(`${request.method} `, "ascii"),
729:       target,
730:       Buffer.from(
731:         ` HTTP/1.1\r\n${lines.map(({ name, value }) => `${name}: ${value}`).join("\r\n")}\r\n\r\n`,
732:         "latin1",
733:       ),
734:     ]);

737:       head,
738:       request.method,
739:       signal,
740:       requestDeadline(signal, deadline),
741:       maximumHeaderBytes,
742:       maximumBodyBytes,
743:       body,
744:       (next) => {

746:       },
747:     );

752:       request.method,
753:       maximumHeaderBytes,
754:       maximumBodyBytes,

760:       bodyOctets: body?.length ?? 0,

764:       writeState: exchanged.writeState,

768:       ...parsed,

770:       exactRequest,

774:         // Ordered exactRequest.headerLines is authoritative for every occurrence.
775:         headers: Object.freeze(

778:       },

781:     return response;
782:   } catch (error) {
783:     if (error instanceof HttpTransportError)
784:       throw new HttpTransportError(error.category, error.message, {}, state);
785:     throw new HttpTransportError(
786:       "configuration",
787:       "exact HTTP request configuration is invalid",
788:       {},
789:       state,
790:     );
791:   }

794: function snapshotDialRoute(route: RawHttpDialRoute): RawHttpDialRoute {
795:   if (route.transport !== "plain" && route.transport !== "tls")
796:     throw new TypeError("dialRoute.transport must be 'plain' or 'tls'");
797:   if (typeof route.host !== "string" || route.host.length === 0)
798:     throw new TypeError("dialRoute.host must be a non-empty string");
799:   if (!Number.isSafeInteger(route.port) || route.port < 1 || route.port > 65_535)
800:     throw new RangeError("dialRoute.port must be an integer from 1 to 65535");
801:   if (route.transport === "plain") return Object.freeze({ ...route });
802:   if (
803:     route.servername !== undefined &&
804:     (typeof route.servername !== "string" || route.servername.length === 0)
805:   )
806:     throw new TypeError("dialRoute.servername must be a non-empty string when present");
807:   if (route.ca !== undefined && (typeof route.ca !== "string" || route.ca.length === 0))
808:     throw new TypeError("dialRoute.ca must be a non-empty string when present");
809:   return Object.freeze({ ...route });
810: }

822: function parseExecutorUrl(value: string): URL {
823:   try {
824:     const url = new URL(value);
825:     if (url.protocol !== "http:" && url.protocol !== "https:") throw new Error("unsupported");
826:     if (url.username !== "" || url.password !== "") throw new Error("credentials");
827:     return url;
828:   } catch (error) {
829:     throw new HttpTransportError("configuration", "HTTP request URL was invalid", {
830:       cause: error,

835: function encodeRequestTarget(rawRequestTarget: Uint8Array | undefined, url: URL): Buffer {
836:   if (rawRequestTarget === undefined) return Buffer.from(`${url.pathname}${url.search}`, "latin1");
837:   if (!(rawRequestTarget instanceof Uint8Array))
838:     throw new HttpTransportError("configuration", "exact raw request target must be a Uint8Array");
839:   if (rawRequestTarget.byteLength > MAXIMUM_RAW_REQUEST_TARGET_BYTES)
840:     throw new HttpTransportError(
841:       "configuration",
842:       `exact raw request target exceeded ${MAXIMUM_RAW_REQUEST_TARGET_BYTES} bytes`,
843:     );
844:   if (rawRequestTarget.some((octet) => octet === 0x20 || octet === 0x0d || octet === 0x0a))
845:     throw new HttpTransportError(
846:       "configuration",
847:       "exact raw request target contained SP, CR, or LF",
848:     );
849:   return Buffer.from(rawRequestTarget);
850: }

852: function effectiveHeaders(
853:   request: HttpExchangeRequest,
854:   authorize: ((request: HttpExchangeRequest) => Readonly<Record<string, string>>) | undefined,
855: ): Headers {

862:     if (!headers.has("accept-encoding")) headers.set("accept-encoding", "identity");
863:     if (!headers.has("user-agent")) headers.set("user-agent", "bdp-conformance/0");
864:     return headers;
865:   } catch (error) {

867:       cause: error,

873:   return name
874:     .split("-")
875:     .map((part) => `${part.slice(0, 1).toUpperCase()}${part.slice(1)}`)
