879: function exchangeRawBytes(
880:   route: RawHttpDialRoute,
881:   requestHead: Uint8Array,
882:   method: HttpMethod,
883:   signal: AbortSignal,
884:   requestTimeoutMs: number,
885:   maximumHeaderBytes: number,
886:   maximumBodyBytes: number,
887:   requestBody?: Uint8Array,
888:   observeWrite?: (state: RequestWriteState) => void,
889: ): Promise<{ bytes: Buffer; writeState: RequestWriteState }> {
890:   return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
891:     let socket: Socket | undefined;
892:     let settled = false;
893:     let earlyCleanup: ReturnType<typeof setImmediate> | undefined;
894:     let connected = false;
895:     let state: RequestWriteState = "not-started";
896:     let completedWrites = 0;
897:     const requiredWrites = requestBody === undefined ? 1 : 2;
898:     const wireLimit = Math.min(Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER, maximumBodyBytes + maximumHeaderBytes * 2);
899:     let buffered = Buffer.alloc(Math.min(8192, wireLimit));
900:     let total = 0;
901:     const raw = (): Buffer => buffered.subarray(0, total);
902:     const recordState = (next: RequestWriteState): void => {
903:       if (settled) return;
904:       state = next;
905:       observeWrite?.(next);
906:     };
907:     const finish = (error?: HttpTransportError): void => {
908:       if (settled) return;
909:       settled = true;
910:       clearTimeout(timer);
911:       if (earlyCleanup !== undefined) clearImmediate(earlyCleanup);
912:       signal.removeEventListener("abort", onAbort);
913:       if (socket !== undefined) {
914:         socket.removeAllListeners();
915:         socket.on("error", () => undefined);
916:         socket.destroy();
917:       }
918:       if (error !== undefined) reject(error);
919:       else resolve({ bytes: Buffer.from(raw()), writeState: state });
920:       buffered = Buffer.alloc(0);
921:     };
922:     const completeResponse = (): boolean => {
923:       try {
924:         return isFramedResponseComplete(raw(), method, maximumHeaderBytes, maximumBodyBytes);
925:       } catch {
926:         return false;
927:       }
928:     };
929:     const writeDone = (error?: Error | null): void => {
930:       if (settled) return;
931:       if (error !== undefined && error !== null) {
932:         finish(
933:           completeResponse()
934:             ? undefined
935:             : new HttpTransportError("disconnect", "HTTP request write disconnected"),
936:         );
937:         return;
938:       }
939:       completedWrites++;
940:       if (completedWrites === requiredWrites) recordState("complete");
941:     };
942:     const onAbort = (): void =>
943:       finish(new HttpTransportError("abort", "HTTP exchange was aborted"));
944:     const timer = setTimeout(
945:       () => finish(new HttpTransportError("timeout", "HTTP exchange timed out")),
946:       requestTimeoutMs,
947:     );
948:     if (signal.aborted) {
949:       onAbort();
950:       return;
951:     }
952:     signal.addEventListener("abort", onAbort, { once: true });
953:     try {
954:       socket =
955:         route.transport === "plain"
956:           ? connectPlain({ host: route.host, port: route.port })
957:           : connectTls({
958:               host: route.host,
959:               port: route.port,
960:               ...(route.servername === undefined ? {} : { servername: route.servername }),
961:               ...(route.ca === undefined ? {} : { ca: route.ca }),
962:             });
963:     } catch {
964:       finish(new HttpTransportError("configuration", "HTTP dial route was invalid"));
965:       return;
966:     }
967:     const peer = socket;
968:     peer.once(route.transport === "plain" ? "connect" : "secureConnect", () => {
969:       if (settled) return;
970:       connected = true;
971:       recordState("started-completion-unestablished");
972:       try {
973:         // Bounded owned buffers; two writes avoid copying a large entity again.
974:         peer.write(requestHead, writeDone);
975:         if (requestBody !== undefined) peer.write(requestBody, writeDone);
976:       } catch {
977:         finish(
978:           completeResponse()
979:             ? undefined
980:             : new HttpTransportError("disconnect", "HTTP request write disconnected"),
981:         );
982:       }
983:     });
984:     peer.on("data", (chunk: Buffer) => {
985:       if (settled) return;
986:       if (chunk.length > wireLimit - total) {
987:         finish(
988:           new HttpTransportError("body-limit", "HTTP response exceeded the configured body limit"),
989:         );
990:         return;
991:       }
992:       const next = total + chunk.length;
993:       if (next > buffered.length) {
994:         const grown = Buffer.alloc(Math.min(wireLimit, Math.max(next, buffered.length * 2)));
995:         buffered.copy(grown, 0, 0, total);
996:         buffered = grown;
997:       }
998:       chunk.copy(buffered, total);
999:       total = next;
1000:       try {
1001:         const head = locateFinalResponseHead(raw(), maximumHeaderBytes);
1002:         if (head === undefined) return;
1003:         if (total - head.headerEnd - 4 > maximumBodyBytes + maximumHeaderBytes)
1004:           throw new HttpTransportError(
1005:             "body-limit",
1006:             "HTTP response exceeded the configured body limit",
1007:           );
1008:         if (isFramedResponseComplete(raw(), method, maximumHeaderBytes, maximumBodyBytes)) {
1009:           if (observeWrite !== undefined && state === "started-completion-unestablished") {
1010:             // A queued body cannot be drained through a peer that has already refused it.
1011:             // Take one cleanup turn, validate all bytes received by then, and terminate this
1012:             // exact early-response exchange; do not manufacture deadline-time success.
1013:             earlyCleanup ??= setImmediate(() => {
1014:               earlyCleanup = undefined;
1015:               if (settled) return;
1016:               try {
1017:                 // Native write completion may arrive after cleanup was scheduled.
1018:                 // Completed requests retain the FIN/trailing-byte observation path.
1019:                 if (state === "complete") {
1020:                   if (!peer.writableEnded) peer.end();
1021:                   return;
1022:                 }
1023:                 parseRawResponse(raw(), method, maximumHeaderBytes, maximumBodyBytes);
1024:                 finish();
1025:               } catch (error) {
1026:                 finish(
1027:                   error instanceof HttpTransportError
1028:                     ? error
1029:                     : new HttpTransportError("invalid-body", "HTTP response framing was malformed"),
1030:                 );
1031:               }
1032:             });
1033:           } else if (state === "complete" && !peer.writableEnded) {
1034:             // Ordinary and fully written requests retain FIN/trailing-byte observation.
1035:             peer.end();
1036:           }
1037:         }
1038:       } catch (error) {
1039:         finish(
1040:           error instanceof HttpTransportError
1041:             ? error
1042:             : new HttpTransportError("invalid-body", "HTTP response framing was malformed"),
1043:         );
1044:       }
1045:     });
1046:     peer.once("end", () => {
1047:       try {
1048:         parseRawResponse(raw(), method, maximumHeaderBytes, maximumBodyBytes);
1049:         finish();
1050:       } catch (error) {
1051:         finish(
1052:           error instanceof HttpTransportError
1053:             ? error
1054:             : new HttpTransportError("invalid-body", "HTTP response framing was malformed"),
1055:         );
1056:       }
1057:     });
1058:     peer.once("error", () =>
1059:       finish(
1060:         completeResponse()
1061:           ? undefined
1062:           : new HttpTransportError(
1063:               connected ? "disconnect" : "network",
1064:               connected ? "HTTP response disconnected" : "HTTP exchange failed",
1065:             ),
1066:       ),
1067:     );
1068:     peer.once("close", () => {
1069:       if (!settled)
1070:         finish(
1071:           completeResponse()
1072:             ? undefined
1073:             : new HttpTransportError(
1074:                 connected ? "disconnect" : "network",
1075:                 connected ? "HTTP response disconnected" : "HTTP exchange failed",
1076:               ),
1077:         );
1078:     });
1079:   });
1080: }

===== ACTUAL SOURCE packages/conformance/src/http-executor.ts:1170-1280 =====
1170: 
1171: function parseRawResponse(
1172:   raw: Buffer,
1173:   method: HttpExchangeRequest["method"],
1174:   maximumHeaderBytes: number,
1175:   maximumBodyBytes: number,
1176: ): {
1177:   readonly status: number;
1178:   readonly headers: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
1179:   readonly bodyText: string;
1180:   readonly bodyOctets: number;
1181: } {
1182:   const head = locateFinalResponseHead(raw, maximumHeaderBytes);
1183:   if (head === undefined)
1184:     throw new HttpTransportError(
1185:       "disconnect",
1186:       "HTTP response disconnected before a final response",
1187:     );
1188:   const { headerEnd, status, headers } = head;
1189:   const framed = raw.subarray(headerEnd + 4);
1190:   const body =
1191:     method === "HEAD" || status === 204 || status === 304
1192:       ? framed
1193:       : decodeResponseBody(framed, headers, maximumBodyBytes);
1194:   if (body.byteLength > maximumBodyBytes)
1195:     throw new HttpTransportError("body-limit", "HTTP response exceeded the configured body limit");
1196:   let bodyText: string;
1197:   try {
1198:     bodyText = new TextDecoder("utf-8", { fatal: true, ignoreBOM: true }).decode(body);
1199:   } catch (error) {
1200:     throw new HttpTransportError("invalid-body", "HTTP response was not valid UTF-8", {
1201:       cause: error,
1202:     });
1203:   }
1204:   return { status, headers, bodyText, bodyOctets: body.byteLength };
1205: }
1206: 
1207: function decodeResponseBody(
1208:   framed: Buffer,
1209:   headers: Readonly<Record<string, string>>,
1210:   maximumBodyBytes: number,
1211: ): Buffer {
1212:   const transferEncoding = headers["transfer-encoding"]?.toLowerCase();
1213:   const contentLength = headers["content-length"];
1214:   if (transferEncoding !== undefined && contentLength !== undefined)
1215:     throw new HttpTransportError("invalid-body", "HTTP response framing was ambiguous");
1216:   if (transferEncoding !== undefined) {
1217:     if (transferEncoding !== "chunked")
1218:       throw new HttpTransportError("invalid-body", "HTTP response transfer coding was unsupported");
1219:     return decodeChunkedBody(framed, maximumBodyBytes);
1220:   }
1221:   if (contentLength === undefined) return framed;
1222:   if (!/^(?:0|[1-9][0-9]*)$/.test(contentLength))
1223:     throw new HttpTransportError("invalid-body", "HTTP response Content-Length was malformed");
1224:   const expected = Number(contentLength);
1225:   if (!Number.isSafeInteger(expected))
1226:     throw new HttpTransportError("body-limit", "HTTP response Content-Length exceeded safe bounds");
1227:   if (expected > maximumBodyBytes)
1228:     throw new HttpTransportError("body-limit", "HTTP response exceeded the configured body limit");
1229:   if (framed.byteLength < expected)
1230:     throw new HttpTransportError(
1231:       "disconnect",
1232:       "HTTP response disconnected before its body was read",
1233:     );
1234:   if (framed.byteLength > expected)
1235:     throw new HttpTransportError(
1236:       "invalid-body",
1237:       "HTTP response contained bytes beyond Content-Length",
1238:     );
1239:   return framed;
1240: }
1241: 
1242: function decodeChunkedBody(framed: Buffer, maximumBodyBytes: number): Buffer {
1243:   const chunks: Buffer[] = [];
1244:   let offset = 0;
1245:   let total = 0;
1246:   while (true) {
1247:     const lineEnd = framed.indexOf("\r\n", offset, "latin1");
1248:     if (lineEnd === -1)
1249:       throw new HttpTransportError("disconnect", "chunked HTTP response ended before a size line");
1250:     const sizeText = framed.subarray(offset, lineEnd).toString("latin1").split(";", 1)[0] ?? "";
1251:     if (!/^[0-9A-Fa-f]+$/.test(sizeText))
1252:       throw new HttpTransportError("invalid-body", "chunked HTTP response size was malformed");
1253:     const size = Number.parseInt(sizeText, 16);
1254:     if (!Number.isSafeInteger(size))
1255:       throw new HttpTransportError("body-limit", "chunked HTTP response size exceeded safe bounds");
1256:     offset = lineEnd + 2;
1257:     if (size === 0) {
1258:       validateChunkedTrailers(framed.subarray(offset));
1259:       return Buffer.concat(chunks);
1260:     }
1261:     total += size;
1262:     if (total > maximumBodyBytes)
1263:       throw new HttpTransportError(
1264:         "body-limit",
1265:         "HTTP response exceeded the configured body limit",
1266:       );
1267:     const end = offset + size;
1268:     if (end + 2 > framed.byteLength)
1269:       throw new HttpTransportError("disconnect", "chunked HTTP response ended inside a chunk");
1270:     if (framed.subarray(end, end + 2).toString("latin1") !== "\r\n")
1271:       throw new HttpTransportError("invalid-body", "chunked HTTP response delimiter was malformed");
1272:     chunks.push(framed.subarray(offset, end));
1273:     offset = end + 2;
1274:   }
1275: }
1276: 
1277: function validateChunkedTrailers(trailers: Buffer): void {
1278:   if (trailers.subarray(0, 2).toString("latin1") === "\r\n") {
1279:     if (trailers.byteLength === 2) return;
1280:     throw new HttpTransportError("invalid-body", "chunked HTTP response contained trailing bytes");

===== ACTUAL SOURCE packages/server/src/transaction-installed.ts:1-300 =====
1: /** Private installed Transactional candidate; never a profile admission API. */
2: import { captureInstalledTransactionConfiguration } from "./transaction-installed-configuration.js";
3: import { installedPayloadIdentity } from "./installed-payload.js";
4: import { startTransactionRuntime } from "./transaction-runtime.js";
5: export async function startInstalledTransactionCandidate(input: unknown) {
6:   const captured = captureInstalledTransactionConfiguration(input);
7:   const payloadIdentity = await installedPayloadIdentity();
8:   const runtime = await startTransactionRuntime(captured);
9:   try {
10:     const metadata = runtime.configurationMetadata;
11:     if (!metadata || metadata.authorityMode !== "canonical" || metadata.scope !== captured.scope)
12:       throw new TypeError("installed transaction metadata unavailable");
13:     return Object.freeze({ ...runtime, configurationMetadata: metadata, payloadIdentity });
14:   } catch (error) {
15:     try {
16:       await runtime.close();
17:     } catch (cleanup) {
18:       throw new AggregateError([error, cleanup], "installed transaction startup cleanup failed");
19:     }
20:     throw error;
21:   }
22: }

===== ACTUAL SOURCE packages/server/src/transaction-installed-configuration.ts:1-300 =====
1: /** Private bounded launch data; compilation remains in the one runtime factory. */
2: import path from "node:path";
3: import { parseCanonicalScope } from "@bdp/protocol";
4: import { administrativeArray, administrativeData } from "./development-contracts.js";
5: import { CONTRACT_ARTIFACT_CEILINGS } from "./installed-contract-artifact.js";
6: import type { TransactionRuntimeOptions } from "./transaction-runtime.js";
7: function text(value: unknown, maximum: number): string {
8:   if (
9:     typeof value !== "string" ||
10:     !value.length ||
11:     !value.isWellFormed() ||
12:     Buffer.byteLength(value) > maximum
13:   )
14:     throw new TypeError("bounded installed transaction string required");
15:   return value;
16: }
17: export function captureInstalledTransactionConfiguration(
18:   input: unknown,
19: ): TransactionRuntimeOptions {
20:   const own = administrativeData(input, [
21:     "scope",
22:     "directory",
23:     "create",
24:     "credentials",
25:     "artifacts",
26:   ]);
27:   const scope = parseCanonicalScope(text(own.scope, 8192));
28:   const directory = text(own.directory, 16384);
29:   if (!path.isAbsolute(directory) || typeof own.create !== "boolean")
30:     throw new TypeError("explicit provisioning required");
31:   const credentials = administrativeArray(own.credentials, 32).map((v) => {
32:     const c = administrativeData(v, ["token", "principal", "canMutate"]);
33:     const token = text(c.token, 256),
34:       principal = text(c.principal, 16384);
35:     if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{32,256}$/.test(token) || typeof c.canMutate !== "boolean")
36:       throw new TypeError("invalid installed credential");
37:     return Object.freeze({ token, principal, canMutate: c.canMutate });
38:   });
39:   if (!credentials.length || new Set(credentials.map((c) => c.token)).size !== credentials.length)
40:     throw new TypeError("unique credentials required");
41:   const supplied = administrativeData(own.artifacts, ["descriptors", "schemas"]);
42:   let total = 0;
43:   const artifacts = (value: unknown) =>
44:     Object.freeze(
45:       administrativeArray(value, CONTRACT_ARTIFACT_CEILINGS.submittedArtifacts).map((v) => {
46:         const a = administrativeData(v, ["retrievalUri", "utf8"]);
47:         const retrievalUri = text(a.retrievalUri, 16384),
48:           utf8 = text(a.utf8, CONTRACT_ARTIFACT_CEILINGS.artifactBytes);
49:         total += Buffer.byteLength(retrievalUri) + Buffer.byteLength(utf8);
50:         if (total > CONTRACT_ARTIFACT_CEILINGS.totalBytes)
51:           throw new TypeError("installed artifact capture bound");
52:         return Object.freeze({ retrievalUri, utf8 });
53:       }),
54:     );
55:   const descriptors = artifacts(supplied.descriptors),
56:     schemas = artifacts(supplied.schemas);
57:   if (!descriptors.length) throw new TypeError("compiled descriptor closure required");
58:   return Object.freeze({
59:     scope,
60:     directory,
61:     create: own.create,
62:     port: 0,
63:     authorityMode: "canonical",
64:     credentials: Object.freeze(credentials),
65:     artifacts: Object.freeze({ descriptors, schemas, limits: CONTRACT_ARTIFACT_CEILINGS }),
66:   });
67: }

===== ACTUAL SOURCE docs/specs/bdp.md:1880-1910 =====
1880: or advertise generic BDP replication. Generic pre-removal administrative reports,
1881: preview endpoints, minimum retention promises, exact-byte witnesses, sync hints,
1882: extra scheme mappings, erased-row enumeration, bulk checks and the alternative
1883: surviving-citation lifecycle remain deferred. Local policy/tooling is allowed;
1884: none weakens required Gone evidence, erasure or incident-Link refusal.
1885: 
1886: ### Problem details
1887: 
1888: Except for the `405` method rejections and unexpected internal `500`
1889: responses defined below, every unsuccessful BDP response uses RFC 9457
1890: Problem Details. BDP defines a small set of stable problem-type families. The
1891: required `code` member identifies the exact normative condition within its
1892: family. Each code fixes its HTTP status and retry disposition. The Read
1893: profile uses this closed table:
1894: 
1895: | Code | Family suffix | HTTP status | Retry |
1896: | --- | --- | --- | --- |
1897: | `malformed-request` | `request` | 400 | `never` |
1898: | `invalid-parameter` | `request` | 400 | `never` |
1899: | `unauthenticated` | `authentication` | 401 | `after-state-change` |
1900: | `forbidden` | `authorization` | 403 | `after-state-change` |
1901: | `resource-not-found` | `not-found` | 404 | `after-state-change` |
1902: | `resource-pruned` | `gone` | 410 | `never` |
1903: | `resource-erased` | `gone` | 410 | `never` |
1904: | `foreign-view` | `conflict` | 409 | `after-state-change` |
1905: | `cursor-expired` | `gone` | 410 | `after-state-change` |
1906: | `request-too-large` | `size` | 413 | `never` |
1907: | `limit-exceeded` | `size` | 413 | `never` |
1908: | `rate-limited` | `rate-limit` | 429 | `after-delay` |
1909: | `temporarily-unavailable` | `unavailable` | 503 | `after-delay` |
1910: | `revision-unknown` | `not-found` | 404 | `after-state-change` |

===== ACTUAL SOURCE docs/specs/bdp.md:5785-5860 =====
5785: BDP v0 assumes a global retention window rather than per-client retention
5786: pins.
5787: 
5788: Ordinary reads are strict by default. Each read observes one
5789: transaction-consistent prefix of its Authorization View that can be
5790: linearized during the request, and it reports its Scope epoch, view token,
5791: and visible position. A client may require a minimum checkpoint bound to that
5792: same view. A replica that is behind must route, wait, or fail explicitly; it
5793: must not return older state as if current. Weaker consistency modes, if
5794: added, require explicit client selection.
5795: 
5796: ### Transactional HTTP consistency
5797: 
5798: Transactional Scope-bounded responses carry:
5799: 
5800: ```http
5801: BDP-Scope-Epoch: opaque-scope-epoch
5802: BDP-Authorization-View: opaque-authorization-view
5803: BDP-Scope-Position: opaque-position-42
5804: ```
5805: 
5806: On reads, a Transactional client requests a minimum visible position by sending those
5807: same epoch and view fields plus:
5808: 
5809: ```http
5810: BDP-Minimum-Scope-Position: opaque-position-42
5811: ```
5812: 
5813: The authority then does one of three things: it returns a representation at
5814: that position or later, it waits or routes to an eligible replica, or it
5815: returns the normative foreign-view, cursor-expired, or catch-up-timeout
5816: problem. It never reports success with an older position.
5817: 
5818: Transactional mutation POSTs follow the shared
5819: [mutation command preconditions](#mutation-command-preconditions): copied read
5820: minimum fields impose no barrier or command-identity input. This does not change
5821: minimum controls on reads, including subsequent Mutation Receipt GET or HEAD
5822: requests.
5823: 
5824: SSE responses use `Cache-Control: no-store, no-transform`.
5825: Intermediaries must not cache or transform the stream.
5826: 
5827: ### Transactional Resource read context
5828: 
5829: Every Transactional Scope-bounded read reports the Scope epoch, Authorization
5830: View token, and position of the transaction-consistent projected prefix it
5831: observed. It reports them through the fields defined under
5832: [Transactional HTTP consistency](#transactional-http-consistency). Read and Read+Update use Resource
5833: revisions, entity tags, and snapshot-preserving pagination without exposing
5834: Scope-history tokens.
5835: 
5836: ### Transactional conditional reads
5837: 
5838: A `304` is not delivery or application of a change group or erasure record
5839: and MUST NOT advance a client's durable checkpoint.
5840: 
5841: Scope epoch/view, minimum-position and erasure/expiry checks MUST precede any
5842: conditional shortcut. Required current Scope context remains required on
5843: 304 and 412 responses.
5844: 
5845: The inherited HEAD rules also cover `changes/`, Scope `events/`, Resource
5846: `view=events`, snapshot handles/streams and receipt/page URLs. For acceptable
5847: SSE, HEAD returns the response metadata and ends without frames; it MUST NOT
5848: remain open merely to stream events. HEAD acknowledges neither delivery nor
5849: application and creates no mutation transaction.
5850: 
5851: ### Transactional HTTP field support
5852: 
5853: The inherited [Mutation command preconditions](#mutation-command-preconditions)
5854: also apply here: HTTP conditional fields on operation command targets do not
5855: guard newly produced receipts.
5856: 
5857: When cross-origin access is enabled, the CORS policy MUST allow `Last-Event-ID`
5858: and the Transactional minimum-position fields, and MUST expose the three
5859: Transactional response fields under the inherited CORS rules.
5860:
